written in CI vectors:
$$+1 \cdot{} \left|0220\right| \cdot{} \left|aaaa\right| +1 \cdot{} \left|aaaa\right| \cdot{} \left|0220\right|$$monomer determinants:
\begin{multline*}+1 \cdot{} \left| \left(\right)\left(b_{1u}^{r}b_{2g}^{r}b_{3g}^{r}a_{u}^{r}\right)\right| +1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{1u}^{l}b_{2g}^{l}b_{3g}^{l}a_{u}^{l}\right)\left(\right)\right|\end{multline*}multiplied out:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+1 \cdot{} \left| \left(\right)\left(-a_{g}+b_{1u}-b_{3u}+b_{2g}-b_{2u}+b_{3g}-b_{1g}+a_{u}\right)\right| +1 \cdot{} \left| \left(+a_{g}+b_{1u}+b_{3u}+b_{2g}+b_{2u}+b_{3g}+b_{1g}+a_{u}\right)\left(\right)\right|
\end{array}\end{multline*}\begin{multline*}+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g}_{a_{g}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}a_{u}_{b_{1u}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{a_{g}b_{3u}b_{3g}b_{1g}_{b_{1u}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{a_{g}b_{3u}b_{3g}a_{u}_{a_{g}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{a_{g}b_{2g}b_{2u}b_{1g}_{b_{1u}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{1u}b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g}_{b_{1u}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{1u}b_{3u}b_{2u}a_{u}_{a_{g}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{1u}b_{3u}b_{3g}b_{1g}_{a_{g}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{1u}b_{3u}b_{3g}a_{u}_{b_{1u}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{1u}b_{2g}b_{2u}b_{1g}_{a_{g}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{2u}a_{u}b_{2u}a_{u}_{a_{g}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{2u}a_{u}b_{3g}b_{1g}_{a_{g}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{2u}a_{u}b_{3g}a_{u}_{b_{1u}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{3g}b_{1g}b_{2u}b_{1g}_{b_{1u}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{3g}b_{1g}b_{2u}a_{u}_{a_{g}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{3g}b_{1g}b_{3g}b_{1g}_{a_{g}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{3g}b_{1g}b_{3g}a_{u}_{b_{1u}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{3g}a_{u}b_{2u}b_{1g}_{a_{g}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{3g}a_{u}b_{2u}a_{u}_{b_{1u}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{3g}a_{u}b_{3g}b_{1g}_{b_{1u}}\right|+1 \cdot{} \left| \underbrace{b_{3g}a_{u}b_{3g}a_{u}_{a_{g}}\right|\end{multline*}
